import scala.util.{Using, Try}\nclass Res(val name: String) extends AutoCloseable {\n  println(s"open $name")\n  def read = s"data of $name"\n  def close(): Unit = println(s"close $name")\n}\nobject T extends App {\n  println(Using(new Res("a"))(_.read))\n  println(Using(new Res("b")) { r => throw new RuntimeException("boom"); r.read })\n  Using.resource(new Res("c")) { r => println(r.read) }\n  def twice(x: => Int) = x + x\n  var n = 0\n  println(twice { n += 1; n }); println(n)\n  def unless(c: Boolean)(body: => Unit) = if (!c) body\n  unless(false) { println("ran") }; unless(true) { println("never") }\n}	open a\nclose a\nSuccess(data of a)\nopen b\nclose b\nFailure(java.lang.RuntimeException: boom)\nopen c\ndata of c\nclose c\n3\n2\nran\n
